). Hence, one has

(5.4)

h,\tilde{h}\geq 6\pi C^{2}\text{ and }\lvert\nabla h\rvert_{G},\lvert\nabla\tilde{h}\rvert_{G}\lesssim\varepsilon

for small enough
\varepsilon>0
, which were the only properties needed to prove the above lemma in the scalar field case.
∎

Lemma 5.3

(Odd order elliptic estimates for
\mathcal{L}
and
\tilde{\mathcal{L}}
)
.

Consider scalar functions
\zeta,Z
with
\mathcal{L}\zeta=Z
or
\tilde{\mathcal{L}}\zeta=Z
. Then, one has

(5.5)

a^{4}\|\nabla\Delta\zeta\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M)}+a^{2}\|\Delta\zeta\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M)}+\|\nabla\zeta\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M)}^{2}\lesssim\|Z\|_{\dot{H}^{1}_{G}(M)}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}a^{-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|Z\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M)}

Proof.

We only prove the statement for
\mathcal{L}
, the proof is identical for
\tilde{\mathcal{L}}
. First, note that

\nabla Z=\nabla(\mathcal{L}\zeta)=a^{4}\nabla\Delta\zeta-h\nabla\zeta+\zeta\nabla h.

Contracting
\nabla_{i}\mathcal{L}\zeta
with
-\nabla^{\sharp i}\zeta
and integrating, we thus obtain

\displaystyle\int_{M}a^{4}\lvert\Delta\zeta\rvert_{G}^{2}+h\lvert\nabla\zeta\rvert^{2}-\zeta\langle\nabla h,\nabla\zeta\rangle_{G}\,{\text{{vol}}}_{G}=-\int_{M}\langle\nabla Z,\nabla\zeta\rangle_{G}\,{\text{{vol}}}_{G}\,.

Using integration by parts to rewrite the third term on the left, applying (
5.4
) and rearranging then yields

\displaystyle\int_{M}a^{4}\lvert\nabla^{2}\zeta\rvert_{G}^{2}+\lvert\nabla\zeta\rvert_{G}^{2}\,{\text{{vol}}}_{G}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\int_{M}\lvert\nabla Z\rvert_{G}^{2}+\|\Delta h\|_{L^{\infty}_{G}}\lvert\zeta\rvert_{G}^{2}\,{\text{{vol}}}_{G}\,.

Using the a priori estimates (
4.3b
) and (
4.3a
) as well as (
3.13a
) for the Vlasov terms, along with Lemma
5.2
, this then becomes

\displaystyle\int_{M}a^{4}\lvert\nabla^{2}\zeta\rvert_{G}+\lvert\nabla\zeta\rvert_{G}^{2}\,{\text{{vol}}}_{G}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\|Z\|_{\dot{H}^{1}}^{2}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}a^{-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|Z\|_{L^{2}_{G}}^{2}\,.

Now contracting
\nabla_{i}\mathcal{L}\zeta
with
a^{4}\nabla^{\sharp i}\Delta\zeta
and integrating and using integration by parts, one has

\displaystyle\,\int_{M}a^{8}\lvert\nabla\Delta\zeta\rvert_{G}^{2}+ha^{4}\lvert\Delta\zeta\rvert^{2}+a^{4}\langle\nabla h,\nabla\zeta\rangle\Delta\zeta+a^{4}\zeta\langle\nabla h,\nabla\Delta\zeta\rangle_{G}\,{\text{{vol}}}_{G}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\int_{M}a^{8}\lvert\nabla\Delta\zeta\rvert_{G}^{2}+ha^{4}\lvert\Delta\zeta\rvert^{2}-a^{4}\Delta h\cdot\zeta\Delta\zeta\,{\text{{vol}}}_{G}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\int_{M}a^{4}\langle\nabla Z,\nabla\Delta\zeta\rangle_{G}\,{\text{{vol}}}_{G}\,.

Thus, by (
5.4
), the following holds for some
K>0
:

\displaystyle\,\int_{M}a^{8}\lvert\nabla\Delta\zeta\rvert_{G}^{2}+6\pi C^{2}a^{4}\lvert\Delta\zeta\rvert^{2}\,{\text{{vol}}}_{G}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\frac{1}{2}\|Z\|_{\dot{H}^{1}_{G}(M)}^{2}+\int_{M}\frac{1}{2}a^{8}\lvert\nabla\Delta\zeta\rvert_{G}^{2}+K\varepsilon\left(\lvert\zeta\rvert^{2}+a^{4}\lvert\Delta\zeta\rvert^{2}\right)\,{\text{{vol}}}_{G}

The statement then follows for small enough
\varepsilon
by rearranging and combining both bounds.
∎

Lemma 5.4

(Lapse energy estimate)
.

The following estimates hold:

(5.6a)

\displaystyle\,a^{8}\mathfrak{E}^{(2)}(N,\cdot)+a^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(1)}(N,\cdot)+\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(N,\cdot)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon^{2}\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\Sigma,\cdot)+\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\phi,\cdot)+a^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}_{1,0}(f,\cdot)+a^{4-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M)}^{2}\,,

(5.6b)

\displaystyle\,a^{8}\mathfrak{E}^{(2)}(N,\cdot)+a^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(1)}(N,\cdot)+\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(N,\cdot)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,a^{8}\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)+{\varepsilon}a^{8-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M)}^{2}+a^{4-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}_{1,0}(f,\cdot)+a^{4-c\sigma}\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M)}^{2}

For any
L\in\{1,\dots,18\}
, one additionally has:

(5.6c)

\displaystyle\,a^{8}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+2)}(N,\cdot)+a^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+1)}(N,\cdot)+\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(N,\cdot)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon^{2}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,\cdot)+\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\phi,\cdot)+\varepsilon^{2}a^{-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left[\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq\max\{0,L-2\})}(\Sigma,\cdot)+\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq\max\{0,L-2\})}(\phi,\cdot)\right]

\displaystyle\,+\underbrace{\left(\varepsilon^{4}a^{-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}+\varepsilon^{2}a^{2-c\sigma}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-3)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}_{\text{not present for }L\leq 2}+a^{4-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,L}(f,\cdot)

(5.6d)

\displaystyle\,a^{8}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+2)}(N,\cdot)+a^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+1)}(N,\cdot)+\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(N,\cdot)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,a^{8}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)+{\varepsilon}a^{8-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\nabla\phi\|_{H^{L}_{G}(M)}^{2}+a^{4-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,L}(f,\cdot)

Proof.

This proof now proceeds as in the pure scalar field setting (see
[
FU24
, Corollary 5.5, 5.8]
): We commute (
5.2
) with
\Delta^{l}
and
\nabla\Delta^{l}
for
l=\lfloor\frac{L}{2}\rfloor=0,\dots,9
and apply the elliptic estimates from Lemma
5.2
and
5.3
respectively. For the estimates with respect to
\mathcal{L}
, the right hand sides at order
L
are of the form

\sum_{I=1}^{L-1}\nabla^{I}H\ast\nabla^{L-I}N+(N+1)\begin{cases}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}H&L\text{ even}\\
\nabla\Delta^{\lfloor\frac{L}{2}\rfloor}H&L\text{ odd}\end{cases}\,.

Again, the only changes occur when considering Vlasov terms: At high order, we apply Lemma
4.14
to estimate the density and pressure terms by Vlasov energies up to curvature errors. Conversely, low order Vlasov terms are bounded using (
4.21
). Regarding the remaining quantities, we note that
[
FU24
, Lemma 4.5]
fully extends to our setting, allowing us to move between Sobolev norms and energies up to curvature errors at high orders.
∎

5.2.
Energy and norm estimates for the scalar field and remaining spacetime variables

In this section, we collect individual energy and Sobolev norm estimates for the shear as well as scalar field and metric variables. Compared to the estimates in
[
FU24
, Section 6]
and their respective proofs, there are two differences we need to account for: Firstly, we now consider arbitrary (constant) spatial sectional curvature instead of
\kappa=-\frac{1}{9}
, which leads to a slightly more refined argument being necessary to prove Lemma
5.5
. Secondly, Vlasov matter now enters the respective equations and the above lapse estimates. Beyond needing to estimate Vlasov matter terms where they occur, the fact that Vlasov matter enters into the elliptic lapse estimate (
5.6c
) leads to a linear coupling between scalar field and Vlasov matter. To deal with this, we need to introduce an additional time-scaling hierarchy in the total energy along with a hierarchy based on
\varepsilon
-scaling, see Definition (
7.4
). To this end, we additionally derive time-scaled estimates along similar lines as their unscaled analogues, and often distribute both types of weights in anticipation of the total energies in Definition (
7.4
). The proofs of the latter otherwise will follow by the same arguments as in the pure scalar field case, so we will often only focus on how these changes are accounted for in the respective proofs.

Lemma 5.5

(Energy estimates for the scalar field)
.

For
L\in 2\mathbb{Z}_{+},\,L\leq 18
, the following estimate holds:

(5.7a)

\displaystyle\,\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\phi,t)+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\dot{a}(s)a(s)^{3}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+1)}(N,s)+\frac{\dot{a}(s)}{a(s)}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(N,s)\,ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\phi,t_{0})+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left({\varepsilon}a(s)^{-3}+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\phi,s)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left\{\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}a(s)^{-3}\cdot\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,s)+\varepsilon a(s)^{-3}\cdot\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},s)\right.

\displaystyle\,\phantom{+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}}+a(s)^{-2-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-(L+1)\omega}\cdot a(s)^{(L+1)\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L)}_{1,\leq L}(f,s)+a(s)^{-2-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-\frac{\omega}{2}}\cdot a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{2}}\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M_{s})}^{2}

\displaystyle\,\phantom{+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}}\left.+\sqrt{\varepsilon}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\phi,s)+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\cdot\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\Sigma,s)\right\}\,ds

\displaystyle\underbrace{+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\varepsilon a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\cdot\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-4)}(\text{{Ric}},s)\,ds}_{\text{if }L\geq 4}

Similarly, one has

(5.7b)

\displaystyle\,a(t)^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+1)}(\phi,t)+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left\{\dot{a}(s)a(s)^{7}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+2)}(N,s)+\dot{a}(s)a(s)^{3}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+1)}(N,s)\right\}\,ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,a(t_{0})^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+1)}(\phi,t_{0})+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(\varepsilon a(s)^{-3}+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\cdot a(s)^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+1)}(\phi,s)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left\{\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}a(s)^{-3}\cdot\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}a(s)^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+1)}(\Sigma,s)+\left(\varepsilon a(s)^{-3}+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\phi,s)\right.

\displaystyle\,\phantom{+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}a(s)^{-3}\cdot\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,s)+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\cdot\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}a(s)^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(L-1)}(\text{{Ric}},s)

\displaystyle\,\phantom{+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}}+\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{2}}a(s)^{-3}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\cdot\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},s)

\displaystyle\,\phantom{+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}}+a(s)^{-2-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-(L+2)\omega}\cdot a(s)^{4+(L+2)\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+1)}_{1,L+1}(f,s)+a(s)^{2-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-(L+1)\omega}\cdot a(s)^{(L+1)\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L)}_{1,\leq L}(f,s)\,

\displaystyle\,\phantom{+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}}\left.+a(s)^{2-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-\frac{\omega}{2}}\cdot a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{2}}\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M_{s})}^{2}\right\}\,ds

For
L=0
, analogous estimates hold with all energies of order
L-1
or lower dropped from the respective right hand sides.

Proof.

We only sketch how to adapt the proof of (
5.7a
) from that of
[
FU24
, Lemma 6.2]
– analogous adaptations can then be applied to the proof of
[
FU24
, Lemma 6.4]
to obtain (
5.7b
) as well as the low order analogues.

After taking the time derivative, inserting (
2.20p
) and integrating by parts, the following schematic equality holds:

(5.8a)

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\phi,\cdot)\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\int_{M}\left\{6C\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N\cdot\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Psi+3a\langle\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N,\nabla\phi\rangle_{G}\cdot\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Psi\right.

(5.8b)

\displaystyle\,\phantom{\int_{M}}\left.-2Ca\langle\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N,\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\phi\rangle_{G}\right\}-4\dot{a}a^{3}\lvert\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\phi\rvert_{G}^{2}\,{\text{{vol}}}_{G}+\langle\text{error terms}\rangle

Regarding the first term in (
5.8a
), we insert the following zero using (
2.20f
) and (
2.3a
):

\displaystyle 0=

\displaystyle\,\int_{M}\text{{div}}_{G}\left(\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N\cdot\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N\right)\,{\text{{vol}}}_{G}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\int_{M}-\frac{3}{8\pi}\dot{a}a^{3}\lvert\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N\rvert_{G}^{2}-\frac{3}{8\pi}\left(-3\kappa\dot{a}a^{3}+12\pi C^{2}\frac{\dot{a}}{a}+12\pi a^{-2}\left({\rho}_{FLRW}^{Vl}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{FLRW}^{Vl}\right)\right)\lvert\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N\rvert^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-6C\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N\cdot\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Psi+\frac{9}{2}\pi a^{-2}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\left[\left(\rho^{Vl}-{\rho}_{FLRW}^{Vl}\right)-\left(\mathfrak{p}^{Vl}-{\mathfrak{p}}^{Vl}_{FLRW}\right)\right]\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N

\displaystyle\quad+\langle\text{borderline and junk terms}\rangle\,{\text{{vol}}}_{G}

Regarding the first line, the first and third term can be pulled to the left hand side, while the second and fourth can be bounded by
\lesssim a^{-2}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(N,\cdot)
. The first term in the second line precisely cancels the first term in (
5.8a
), and the term containing Vlasov matter can be bounded by

(5.9)

a^{-2-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L)}_{1,\leq L}(f,\cdot)}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(N,\cdot)}\,

by Lemma
4.14
. Note that, for the same argument at
L=0
, this also incurs a metric term.

The second term in (
5.8a
) and the explicit term in (
5.8b
) can be treated similarly, so we focus on the latter, denoting it by
(\ast)
: Applying (
2.5
), we obtain the following:

\displaystyle\lvert(\ast)\rvert\leq

\displaystyle\,\int_{M}2\sqrt{\frac{3}{4\pi}}\left(\dot{a}a^{3}+\kappa a^{3}\right)\lvert\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N\rvert_{G}\lvert\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\phi\rvert_{G}\,{\text{{vol}}}_{G}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\int_{M}\left(4\dot{a}a^{3}\lvert\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\phi\rvert_{G}^{2}+\frac{3}{16\pi}\dot{a}a^{3}\lvert\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N\rvert_{G}^{2}\right)\,{\text{{vol}}}_{G}+\lvert\kappa\rvert a^{-1}\left(\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\phi,\cdot)+a^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+1)}(N,\cdot)\right)

The first term cancels with the second term in (
5.8b
), the second can be pulled to the left and absorbed into already present lapse energy terms after adapting constants.

Combining these observations and dealing with borderline and junk terms as in the pure scalar field setting, one obtains the following differential estimate:

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\phi,\cdot)+\dot{a}a^{3}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+1)}(N,\cdot)+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(N,\cdot)

(5.10a)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\left(\varepsilon a^{-3}+a^{-1-c{\sigma}}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\phi,\cdot)+\left(\varepsilon a^{-3}+a^{-2-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\left(a^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+1)}(N,\cdot)+\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(N,\cdot)\right)

(5.10b)

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon a^{-3}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,\cdot)+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{-3}\mathfrak{E}^{(L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)+{\varepsilon}a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\phi,\cdot)

(5.10c)

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\Sigma,\cdot)+\left[\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right]\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(N,\cdot)

(5.10d)

\displaystyle\,+a^{-2-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L)}_{1,\leq L}(f,\cdot)+\underbrace{\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-4)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}_{\text{not present for }L=2}

The statement then follows by applying Lemma
5.4
to the lapse energies in (
5.10a
) and (
5.10c
) and integrating. We note that, for
L=0
, one not only incurs metric norms by applying elliptic lapse estimates at low orders, but also when estimating (
5.9
).
∎

We will need an additional norm bound to obtain optimal control for the scalar field:

Lemma 5.6

(Norm bound for
\nabla\phi
)
.

\|\nabla\phi\|_{H^{l}_{G}(M)}\lesssim(1+\varepsilon a^{-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}})\|\Sigma\|_{H^{l+1}_{G}(M)}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}a^{-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\Psi\|_{H^{l}_{G}(M)}+(1+\varepsilon a^{-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}})\|\jmath^{Vl}\|_{H^{l}_{G}(M)}

Proof.

The momentum constraint (
2.20i
) leads to the following expression after rearranging and using (
4.1a
) to ensure
\Psi+C>\frac{C}{2}
:

\displaystyle\lvert\nabla^{l}\nabla\phi\rvert_{G}=

\displaystyle\,\left\lvert\nabla^{l}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{8\pi}\text{{div}}_{G}\Sigma-\jmath^{Vl}}{\Psi+C}\right)\right\rvert_{G}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\lvert\nabla^{l+1}\Sigma\rvert_{G}+\lvert\nabla^{l}\jmath^{Vl}\rvert_{G}+\sum_{I_{1}+I_{2}=l-1}\left(\lvert\nabla^{I_{1}+1}\Sigma\rvert_{G}+\lvert\nabla^{I_{1}}\jmath^{Vl}\rvert_{G}\right)\lvert\nabla^{I_{2}+1}\Psi\rvert_{G}

The statement then follows by applying the Young inequality with (
4.3b
), (
4.21
) and (
4.1a
).
∎

Before moving on the energy estimates for Bel-Robinson variables, we prepare the necessary bounds on the Vlasov matter components that occur in the Bel-Robinson evolution equations (
2.20l
) and (
2.20m
):

Lemma 5.7

(Bounds for Bel-Robinson Vlasov matter components)
.

Recalling (
2.20n
) and (
2.20o
), writing

(J^{Vl})^{(\ast)}_{i0j,symm}=\frac{1}{2}\left[(J^{Vl})^{(\ast)}_{i0j}+(J^{Vl})^{(\ast)}_{j0i}\right]

and using
J^{Vl,\parallel}_{(\cdot)0(\cdot),symm}
to denote the same expression as in (
2.20n
) where pure trace terms are dropped, one has

\displaystyle\|a^{4}(N+1)J^{Vl,\parallel}_{(\cdot)0(\cdot),symm}\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M)}

\displaystyle\,\lesssim a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-\frac{\omega}{2}}\sqrt{a^{\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}_{1,0}(f,\cdot)}+a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-\omega}\sqrt{a^{4+2\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(1)}_{1,\leq 1}(f,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{a^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(1)}(N,\cdot)}+a^{1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(N,\cdot)}+a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}

as well as, for
L\in 2\mathbb{Z}_{+},L\leq 18
,

\displaystyle\,\|a^{4}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\left((N+1)J^{Vl,\parallel}_{(\cdot)0(\cdot),symm}\right)\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-\frac{L+2}{2}\omega}\sqrt{a^{4+(L+2)\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+1)}_{1,L+1}(f,\cdot)}+a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-\frac{L+1}{2}\omega}\sqrt{a^{(L+1)\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L)}_{1,\leq L}(f,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{a^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+1)}(N,\cdot)}+a^{1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(N,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\sqrt{\varepsilon}a^{1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(N,\cdot)}+a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\sqrt{\varepsilon}a^{-1-c\sigma}\sqrt{a^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(L-1)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}

and, for
L\in 2\mathbb{N},L\leq 18
,

\displaystyle\,\|a^{4}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\left((N+1)J^{Vl,\ast}_{(\cdot)0(\cdot),symm}\right)\|_{L^{2}_{G}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,a^{-2-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-\frac{(L+2)}{2}\omega}\sqrt{a^{4+(L+2)\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+1)}_{1,L+1}(f,\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-\frac{L+1}{2}\omega}\sqrt{a^{(L+1)\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L)}_{1,L}(f,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}+\underbrace{\varepsilon a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}}_{\text{if }L\neq 0}

Proof.

We can directly use Lemma
4.14
to control
(S^{Vl})^{\parallel}
and
\nabla\jmath^{Vl}
. Additionally, we note that the final term in (
2.20n
) can be estimated identically to
(S^{Vl})^{\parallel}
after observing that
m^{2}a^{2}\leq(v^{0})^{2}
. For the remaining terms, recall that we can apply (
2.23
) to pull the covariant derivative past the respective momentum integrals. By Lemma
2.7
, any of the prefactors depending only on the momentum are uniformly bounded by
v^{0}
up to constant and vanish when hit by a horizontal derivative. Keeping this in mind, we apply the product rule: When estimating high order Vlasov terms, we apply the Jensen inequality as in the proof of Lemma
4.14
to connect the respectice quantities to our Vlasov energies, and apply the low order bounds (
4.3b
) and (
3.13a
) for shear and lapse respectively. On the other hand, if most derivatives hit lapse or shear terms, we estimate the respective distribution function terms with (
4.16
) by
a^{-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}
, and simply apply (
4.6a
) to deal with the remaining integrals over functions of
v
. Finally, recall
[
FU24
, Lemma 4.5]
to bound Sobolev norms in
\Sigma
and
N
to their respective energy terms up to curvature errors.
∎

Corollary 5.8

(Energy estimates for Bel-Robinson variables)
.

The following holds for
L\in 2\mathbb{N},\,2\leq L\leq 18
:

(5.11a)

\displaystyle\,a(t)^{\frac{\omega}{4}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(W,t)+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\int_{M}a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{4}}\left[8\pi C^{2}a(s)^{-3}(N+1)\langle\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Sigma,\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\mathcal{E}\rangle_{G}+6\frac{\dot{a}(s)}{a(s)}\lvert\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\mathcal{E}\rvert_{G}^{2}\right]\,{\text{{vol}}}_{G}\,ds

\displaystyle\,\lesssim a(t_{0})^{\frac{\omega}{4}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(W,t_{0})+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(\varepsilon a(s)^{-3}+a(s)^{-3+\frac{\omega}{8}}+a(s)^{-2-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\,a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{4}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(W,s)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left\{\left(a(s)^{-3+\frac{\omega}{8}}+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\,\left(a(s)^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+1)}(\phi,s)+\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\phi,s)\right)\right.

\displaystyle\,\phantom{\int_{t}^{t_{0}}}+\left(\varepsilon a(s)^{-3}+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\,a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{4}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,s)

\displaystyle\,\phantom{\int_{t}^{t_{0}}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}a(s)^{-3+\frac{\omega}{8}}\,\cdot\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}a(s)^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(L-1)}(\text{{Ric}},s)+\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{2}}a(s)^{-3+\frac{\omega}{8}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\frac{\omega}{8}}\right)\,\cdot\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},s)

\displaystyle\,\phantom{\int_{t}^{t_{0}}}+a(s)^{-2-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-(L+2)\omega}\,\cdot a(s)^{4+(L+2)\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+1)}_{1,L+1}(f,s)+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-(L+1)\omega}\cdot a(s)^{(L+1)\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L)}_{1,\leq L}(f,s)

\displaystyle\,\phantom{\int_{t}^{t_{0}}}+a(s)^{3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-\frac{\omega}{4}}\cdot a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{2}}\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{G}(M_{s})}+\left(\varepsilon^{2}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\frac{\omega}{8}}+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\frac{\omega}{8}}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\phi,s)

\displaystyle\,\phantom{\int_{t}^{t_{0}}}+\varepsilon a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\cdot a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{4}}\left(\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\Sigma,s)+\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(W,s)\right)

\displaystyle\,\phantom{\int_{t}^{t_{0}}}+\underbrace{\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\frac{\omega}{8}}\cdot\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-4)}(\text{{Ric}},s)}_{\text{not present for }L=2}\Bigr\}\,ds\,.

(5.11b)

\displaystyle\,\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(W,t)+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\int_{M}\left[8\pi C^{2}a(s)^{-3}(N+1)\langle\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Sigma,\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\mathcal{E}\rangle_{G}+6\frac{\dot{a}(s)}{a(s)}\lvert\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\mathcal{E}\rvert_{G}^{2}\right]\,{\text{{vol}}}_{G}\,ds

\displaystyle\,\lesssim\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(W,t_{0})+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{8}}a(s)^{-3}+a(s)^{-2-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(W,s)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left\{\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{8}}a(s)^{-3}\cdot a(s)^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+1)}(\phi,s)+\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{8}}a(s)^{-3}+a(s)^{-1}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\phi,s)\right.

\displaystyle\,\phantom{\int_{t}^{t_{0}}}+\left(\varepsilon a(s)^{-3}+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,s)

\displaystyle\,\phantom{\int_{t}^{t_{0}}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{8}}a(s)^{-3}\cdot\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}a(s)^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(L-1)}(\text{{Ric}},s)+\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{27}{8}}a(s)^{-3}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\cdot\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},s)

\displaystyle\,\phantom{\int_{t}^{t_{0}}}+a(s)^{-2-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-(L+2)\omega}\cdot a(s)^{4+(L+2)\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+1)}_{1,L+1}(f,s)+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-(L+1)\omega}\cdot a(s)^{(L+1)\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L)}_{1,\leq L}(f,s)

\displaystyle\,\phantom{\int_{t}^{t_{0}}}+a(s)^{3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-\frac{\omega}{4}}\cdot a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{2}}\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M_{s})}^{2}+\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{15}{8}}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\phi,s)

\displaystyle\,\left.\phantom{\int_{t}^{t_{0}}}+\varepsilon a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left(\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\Sigma,s)+\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(W,s)\right)+\underbrace{\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\cdot\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-4)}(\text{{Ric}},s)}_{\text{not present for }L=2}\right\}\,ds\,.

For
L=0
, the analogues of (
5.11
) hold where all energies of order below
L
are dropped from the right hand sides.

Proof.

The proof proceeds exactly as in the pure scalar field case (see
[
FU24
, Lemma 6.6]
), where we use Lemma
5.7
to bound the Vlasov matter terms, and apply Lemma
5.4
to bound lapse energies.
∎

Lemma 5.9

(Integral energy estimates for the second fundamental form)
.

For
L\in 2\mathbb{Z}_{+},L\leq 18
, the following holds:

(5.12a)

\displaystyle\,a(t)^{\frac{\omega}{4}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,t)+2\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\int_{M}a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{4}}\left[a(s)^{-3}(N+1)\langle\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\mathcal{E},\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Sigma\rangle_{G}+\frac{\dot{a}(s)}{a(s)}(N+1)\lvert\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Sigma\rvert_{G}^{2}\right]\,{\text{{vol}}}_{G}\,ds

\displaystyle\,\lesssim a(t_{0})^{\frac{\omega}{4}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,t_{0})+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(\varepsilon a(s)^{-3}+a(s)^{-3+\frac{\omega}{8}}+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{4}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,s)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\Bigr\{a(s)^{-3+\frac{\omega}{8}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\phi,s)+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}a(s)^{3-c\sigma+\frac{\omega}{4}}\cdot\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L-1)}(\text{{Ric}},s)+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}a(s)^{-3+\frac{\omega}{8}}\cdot\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},s)

\displaystyle\,\phantom{\int_{t}^{t_{0}}}+\varepsilon^{2}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\cdot a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{4}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\Sigma,s)+\varepsilon^{2}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\frac{\omega}{8}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\phi,s)

\displaystyle\,\phantom{\int_{t}^{t_{0}}}+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-(L+1)\omega}\cdot\left(a(s)^{(L+1)\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L)}_{1,\leq L}(f,s)+a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{2}}\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M_{s})}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\,\phantom{\int_{t}^{t_{0}}}+\underbrace{\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\frac{\omega}{8}}\cdot\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-4)}(\text{{Ric}},s)}_{\text{not present for }L=2}\Bigr\}\,ds

(5.12b)

\displaystyle\,\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,t)+2\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\int_{M}\left[a(s)^{-3}(N+1)\langle\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\mathcal{E},\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Sigma\rangle_{G}+\frac{\dot{a}(s)}{a(s)}(N+1)\lvert\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Sigma\rvert_{G}^{2}\right]\,{\text{{vol}}}_{G}\,ds

\displaystyle\,\lesssim\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,t_{0})+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{8}}a(s)^{-3}+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,s)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\Bigr\{\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{8}}a(s)^{-3}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\phi,s)+\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{4}}a(s)^{3-c\sigma}\cdot\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L-1)}(\text{{Ric}},s)+\varepsilon^{2}a(s)^{-3}\mathfrak{E}^{(L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},s)

\displaystyle\,\phantom{\int_{t}^{t_{0}}}+\varepsilon^{2}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\Sigma,s)+\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{15}{8}}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}+\varepsilon a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\phi,s)

\displaystyle\,\phantom{\int_{t}^{t_{0}}}+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-(L+1)\omega}\cdot\left(a(s)^{(L+1)\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L)}_{1,\leq L}(f,s)+a(s)^{(L+1)\omega}\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M_{s})}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\,\phantom{\int_{t}^{t_{0}}}+\underbrace{\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\cdot\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-4)}(\text{{Ric}},s)}_{\text{not present for }L=2}\Bigr\}\,ds

For
L=0
, the same estimate holds with all energies of order
L-1
or lower dropped from the upper bound.

Proof.

Note that the only term that is not pure trace that the presence of Vlasov matter adds to the evolution equation is
S^{Vl,\parallel}
. Hence, commuting (
2.20c
) with
\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}
and carrying over error term notation from
[
FU24
, (11.14)]
, we have the following:

(5.13a)

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,\cdot)=

\displaystyle\,\int_{M}\left\{2a\langle\nabla^{2}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N,\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Sigma\rangle_{G}-2a(N+1)\langle\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\text{{Ric}}[G],\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Sigma\rangle_{G}\right.

(5.13b)

\displaystyle\,\qquad+16\pi a^{-1}\langle\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}S^{Vl,\parallel},\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Sigma\rangle_{G}

(5.13c)

\displaystyle\,\qquad+(\partial_{t}G^{-1})\ast G^{-1}\ast\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Sigma\ast\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Sigma-3N\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\lvert\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Sigma\rvert_{G}^{2}

(5.13d)

\displaystyle\,\qquad\left.-2\langle\mathfrak{S}_{L,Border},\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Sigma\rangle_{G}-2\langle\mathfrak{S}_{L,Junk}^{\parallel},\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Sigma\rangle_{G}\right\}\,{\text{{vol}}}_{G}

Regarding (
5.13a
), we apply the lapse energy estimate (
5.6c
) (resp., for
L=0
, (
5.6a
)) for the first term and have the following:

\displaystyle\,a\|\nabla^{2}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N\|_{L^{2}_{G}}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}\lesssim a^{-3}\sqrt{a^{8}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+2)}(N,\cdot)+\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(N,\cdot)}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{8}}a^{-3}+a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,\cdot)+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{8}}a^{-3}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\phi,\cdot)+a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-(L+1)\omega}\cdot\,a^{(L+1)\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,L}(f,\cdot)

\displaystyle\,+\begin{cases}a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-\frac{\omega}{2}}\cdot a^{\frac{\omega}{2}}\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M)}^{2}&L=0\\
\varepsilon^{\frac{15}{8}}a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left(\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\phi,\cdot)+\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\Sigma,\cdot)\right)+\varepsilon^{\frac{15}{8}}a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-3)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)&L\geq 2\end{cases}

For the second term in (
5.13a
), we insert the Bel-Robinson constraint (
2.20j
). High order lapse terms are treated similarly as above, and the only Vlasov term that is tracefree and thus isn’t cancelled by the inner product is

-2(N+1)a^{-3}\cdot 4\pi a^{2}\langle\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}S^{Vl,\parallel},\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Sigma\rangle_{G}\,.

This term is bounded in absolute value by
a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,L}(f,\cdot)}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}
up to constant with Lemma
4.14
, as is the term in (
5.13b
). (
5.13c
)-(
5.13d
) only contribute error terms that can be dealt with as in the scalar field setting, accounting for (
3.14
).
∎

Lemma 5.10

(Elliptic energy estimate for the second fundamental form)
.

For any
L\in 2\mathbb{Z}_{+}
,
2\leq L\leq 18
, we have

(5.14a)

\displaystyle a^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+1)}(\Sigma,\cdot)

\displaystyle\,\lesssim\left(a^{4-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}+\varepsilon a^{2-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,\cdot)+\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\phi,\cdot)+\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(W,\cdot)

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{4}}\cdot\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}a^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(L-1)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)+a^{4-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-(L+1)\omega}\cdot a^{(L+1)\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,L}(f,\cdot)

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon a^{2-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\phi,\cdot)+a^{2-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\Sigma,\cdot)+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{2-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\cdot\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)\,.

For
L=0
, one analogously has

(5.14b)

a^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(1)}(\Sigma,\cdot)\lesssim\,\left(a^{4-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}+\varepsilon a^{2-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\Sigma,\cdot)+\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\phi,\cdot)+\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(W,\cdot)+a^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}_{1,0}(f,\cdot)\,.

Proof.

Recall from the proof of
[
FU24
, Lemma 6.10]
that, since
\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Sigma
is tracefree, one has

\mathfrak{E}^{(L+1)}(\Sigma,\cdot)\lesssim a^{-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,\cdot)+\int_{M}\lvert\text{{div}}_{G}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Sigma\rvert_{G}^{2}+\lvert\text{{curl}}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Sigma\rvert_{G}^{2}\,{\text{{vol}}}_{G}

Inserting commuted analogues of (
2.20i
) and (
2.20k
), we can argue as in the scalar field setting, where the commuted analogue of (
2.20i
) now additionally contains the term

8\pi\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\jmath^{Vl},

where we again apply Lemma
4.14
, leading to the respective Vlasov energy terms.
∎

We close out this section by collecting all necessary bounds for metric variables:

Lemma 5.11

(Sobolev norm estimates for metric variables)
.

For any
l\in\mathbb{N},\,l\leq 18
, we have the following:

(5.15a)

\displaystyle a(t)^{\frac{\omega}{2}}\|G^{\pm 1}-\gamma^{\pm 1}

\displaystyle\|_{H^{l}_{G}(M_{t})}^{2}\lesssim\varepsilon^{4}+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(a(s)^{-3+\frac{\omega}{8}}+a(s)^{-1-c\sigma}\right)\cdot a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{2}}\|G^{\pm 1}-\gamma^{\pm 1}\|_{H^{l}_{G}(M_{s})}^{2}\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}a(s)^{-3+\frac{\omega}{8}-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left(a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{4}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq l)}(\Sigma,s)+\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq l)}(\phi,s)+\underbrace{\varepsilon\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq l-2)}(\text{{Ric}},s)}_{\text{if }l\geq 3}\right)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-(l+1)\omega}\cdot a(s)^{(l+1)\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq l)}_{1,\leq l}(f,s)\,ds

(5.15b)

\displaystyle\|G^{\pm 1}-\gamma^{\pm 1}

\displaystyle\|_{H^{l}_{G}(M_{t})}^{2}\lesssim a^{-c\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{8}}}\left(\varepsilon^{4}+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{4}}\sup_{s\in(t,t_{0})}\left(\|N\|_{H^{l}_{G}(M_{s})}^{2}+\|\Sigma\|_{H^{l}_{G}(M_{s})}^{2}\right)\right)

For
l>0
, one additionally has

(5.16a)

\displaystyle a(t)^{\beta+\frac{\omega}{2}}

\displaystyle\,\|\Gamma-\hat{\Gamma}\|_{H^{l-1}_{G}(M_{t})}^{2}\lesssim\varepsilon^{4}+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(a(s)^{-3+\frac{\omega}{8}-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}+a(s)^{-1-c\sigma}\right)\cdot a(s)^{\beta+\frac{\omega}{2}}\|\Gamma-\hat{\Gamma}\|_{H^{l-1}_{G}(M_{s})}^{2}\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}a(s)^{-3+\frac{\omega}{8}}\cdot a(s)^{\beta}\left(a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{4}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq l)}(\Sigma,s)+\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq l)}(\phi,s)+\underbrace{\varepsilon\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq l-2)}(\text{{Ric}},s)}_{\text{if }l\geq 3}\right)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-(l+1)\omega}\cdot a(s)^{\beta+(l+1)\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq l)}_{1,\leq l}(f,s)\,ds\,\text{ and}

(5.16b)

\displaystyle\,\|\Gamma-\hat{\Gamma}\|_{H^{l-1}_{G}(M_{t})}^{2}\lesssim a(t)^{-c\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{8}}}\left(\varepsilon^{4}+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{4}}\sup_{s\in(t,t_{0})}\left(\|N\|_{H^{l}_{G}(M_{s})}^{2}+\|\Sigma\|_{H^{l}_{G}(M_{s})}^{2}\right)\right)

as well as the following:

(5.17a)

\displaystyle a(t)^{\frac{\omega}{2}}

\displaystyle\,\mathfrak{E}^{(L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},t)\lesssim a(t_{0})^{\frac{\omega}{4}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},t_{0})

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{8}}a(s)^{-3}+a(s)^{-3+\frac{\omega}{8}}+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{2}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},s)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\Bigr\{a(s)^{-3+\frac{\omega}{8}}\left(\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\phi,s)+a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{4}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,s)\right)

\displaystyle\,\quad+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-(L+1)\omega}\cdot a(s)^{(L+1)\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L)}_{1,\leq L}(f,s)

\displaystyle\,\quad+a(s)^{-3+\frac{\omega}{8}-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left(\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\phi,s)+a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{4}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\Sigma,s)\right)

\displaystyle\,\quad+\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{8}}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\cdot a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{2}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-4)}(\text{{Ric}},s)\Bigr\}\,ds\,,

(5.17b)

\displaystyle a(t)^{4+{\omega}}

\displaystyle\,\mathfrak{E}^{(L-1)}(\text{{Ric}},t)\lesssim\varepsilon^{4}+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{8}}a(s)^{-3}+a(s)^{-3+\frac{\omega}{4}}\right)\cdot a(s)^{4+{\omega}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L-1)}(\text{{Ric}},s)\,ds

\displaystyle+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\Bigr\{

\displaystyle\,a(s)^{-3+\frac{\omega}{4}}\cdot a(s)^{4+\frac{\omega}{2}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+1)}(\Sigma,s)+\left(a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}+\varepsilon^{2}a(s)^{-3+\frac{\omega}{2}}\right)a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{4}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,s)

\displaystyle\,\quad+\left(a(s)^{-3+\frac{3\omega}{4}}+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\phi,s)

\displaystyle\,\quad+\left(a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}+\varepsilon^{2}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\frac{\omega}{2}}\right)\left(\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\phi,s)+a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{4}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\Sigma,s)\right)

\displaystyle\,\quad+a(s)^{-1-c\sigma-(L+1)\omega}\cdot a(s)^{(L+1)\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L)}_{1,\leq L}(f,s)

\displaystyle\,\quad\left.+\varepsilon a(s)^{-3+\frac{\omega}{4}}\cdot\,a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{2}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},s)+\varepsilon a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\frac{\omega}{4}}\,\cdot a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{2}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-4)}(\text{{Ric}},s)\right\}\,ds

(5.17c)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{E}^{(L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},t)\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\mathfrak{E}^{(L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},t_{0})+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{8}}a(s)^{-3}+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{(L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},s)\,ds

\displaystyle+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\Bigr\{

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{8}}a(s)^{-3}\left(\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\phi,s)+\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,s)\right)+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-(L+1)\omega}\cdot a(s)^{(L+1)\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L)}_{1,\leq L}(f,s)

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{8}}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left(\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\phi,s)+\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\Sigma,s)\right)

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{8}}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-4)}(\text{{Ric}},s)\Bigr\}\,ds\,,

(5.17d)

\displaystyle a(t)^{4}

\displaystyle\,\mathfrak{E}^{(L-1)}(\text{{Ric}},t)\lesssim\varepsilon^{4}+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{8}}a(s)^{-3}+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\cdot a(s)^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(L-1)}(\text{{Ric}},s)\,ds

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\,\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left\{\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{8}}a(s)^{-3}\cdot a(s)^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+1)}(\Sigma,s)+\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{15}{8}}a(s)^{-3}+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,s)\right.

\displaystyle\,+\left(\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{8}}a(s)^{-3}+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}(\phi,s)

\displaystyle\,+\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{15}{8}}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\left(\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\phi,s)+\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\Sigma,s)\right)

\displaystyle\,+a(s)^{-1-c\sigma-(L+1)\omega}\cdot a(s)^{(L+1)\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L)}_{1,\leq L}(f,s)

\displaystyle\,\left.+\varepsilon a(s)^{-3}\mathfrak{E}^{(L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},s)+\varepsilon a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-4)}(\text{{Ric}},s)\right\}\,ds

For
L=2
, the previous two estimates extend if all energies with negative order are dropped, while one additionally has the following:

(5.17e)

\displaystyle a(t)^{\frac{\omega}{2}}\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}

\displaystyle\,(\text{{Ric}},t)\lesssim a(t_{0})^{\frac{\omega}{2}}\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\text{{Ric}},t_{0})+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(a(s)^{-3+\frac{\omega}{8}}+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\text{{Ric}},s)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}a(s)^{-3+\frac{\omega}{8}}\left(\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\phi,s)+a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{4}}\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\Sigma,s)\right)

\displaystyle\,\qquad+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-{\omega}}\left(a(s)^{\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}_{1,0}(f,s)+a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{2}}\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M_{s})}^{2}\right)\,ds

(5.17f)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}

\displaystyle\,(\text{{Ric}},t)\lesssim\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\text{{Ric}},t_{0})+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{8}}a(s)^{-3}+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\text{{Ric}},s)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{8}}a(s)^{-3}\left(\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\phi,s)+\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\Sigma,s)\right)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}-\omega}\left(a(s)^{\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}_{1,0}(f,s)+a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{2}}\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M_{s})}^{2}\right)\,ds

Finally, one has the following bound:

(5.17g)

\displaystyle a^{8}\mathfrak{E}^{(l)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\mathfrak{E}^{(l)}(W,\cdot)+\mathfrak{E}^{(l)}(\Sigma,\cdot)+\varepsilon a^{-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq l-2)}(\Sigma,\cdot)+\mathfrak{E}^{(l)}(\phi,\cdot)+\varepsilon a^{-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq l-2)}(\phi,\cdot)

\displaystyle\,+a^{4-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq l)}_{1,\leq l}(f,\cdot)\underbrace{+a^{4-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}^{2}}_{\text{if }l=0}+\underbrace{\varepsilon\mathfrak{E}^{(l-2)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)+\varepsilon a^{-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq l-4)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}_{\text{if }l\geq 4}

Proof.

Since (
2.20a
), (
2.20d
) and (
2.20e
) do not contain Vlasov matter variables, the proofs for (
5.15b
), (
5.16b
) and (
5.17c
)-(
5.17f
) are largely unchanged to those of
[
FU24
, Lemma 6.11 - 6.14]
, where we apply Lemma
5.4
to estimate lapse energy terms. Regarding the scaled estimates, we consider (
5.15a
) for even
l\geq 2
; the other cases as well as (
5.16a
) are proven similarly: Commuting (
2.20a
) with
\Delta^{\frac{l}{2}}
, we have the following:

\displaystyle\,-\partial_{t}\left(a(t)^{\frac{\omega}{2}}\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{H^{l}_{G}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\frac{\omega}{2}}\left(\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq l)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}+\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq l)}(N,\cdot)}+\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq l-2)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}\right)\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{H^{l}_{G}}

We apply the Young inequality to, for example, the shear term as follows:

a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\frac{\omega}{2}}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq l)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{H^{l}_{G}}\lesssim a^{-3+\frac{\omega}{8}}\cdot a^{\frac{\omega}{2}}\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{H^{l}_{G}}^{2}+a^{-3+\frac{\omega}{8}-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\,\cdot a^{\frac{\omega}{4}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq l)}(\Sigma,\cdot)

The statement then follows by inserting Lemma
5.4
, applying the Young inequality similarly to the other terms and integrating. From comparing (
2.20b
) to (
2.20a
), one quickly observes that the analogous bound for the inverse metric follows identically.

Finally, (
5.17g
) is a direct consequence of applying
\Delta^{\frac{l}{2}}
(resp.
\nabla\Delta^{\frac{l-1}{2}}
) to the constraint equation (
2.20j
), rearranging and applying the a priori estimates (
4.1b
), (
4.3b
), (
4.3d
), (
4.1a
) and (
4.3a
). In particular, regarding the Vlasov terms, one uses (
4.27d
) and (
4.27a
) to connect their norms to our energy quantities. For the remaining terms, we use estimates as in
[
FU24
, Lemma 4.5]
, along with the previously mentioned a priori estimates, to convert Sobolev norms into energies up to curvature errors.
∎

6.
Vlasov energy estimates

In this section, we collect the necessary energy estimates to obtain the bootstrap improvements in the following section. In particular, for each derivative order and each number of horizontal derivatives in the energy, we prove both a time-scaled estimate, which are all combined in Lemma
7.3
to obtain bootstrap improvements for the spacetime and scalar field variables, as well as non-scaled estimates that lose smallness, which we use to improve the bootstrap assumptions on Vlasov matter. Regarding the time-scaled estimates, we scale by
a^{\omega}
and powers there of to indicate scaling necessary within the hierarchy of vertical and horizontal derivatives at a given order (foreshadowing (
7.2
)), and by
a^{\beta}
to indicate additional scaling necessary for the total energy at odd derivative orders (foreshadowing the final line of (
7.5c
)).

6.1.
Preliminaries

First, we collect some tools that will be used throughout:

Remark 6.1

(Integrating
M_{t}
-tangent tensors over the tangent space)
.

We recall that by Lemma
4.6
, positive powers of
\lvert v\rvert_{G}
can be bounded by
a^{-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}
, and thus integrals over powers of
\lvert v\rvert_{G}
and
v^{0}
on the support of
f
,
f_{FLRW}
or
f-f_{FLRW}
are bounded by
a^{-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}
. In particular, for any
M_{t}
-tangent tensor
\mathfrak{T}
and any
\alpha>-3
, this implies

\int_{T^{\ast}M_{t}}\lvert\mathfrak{T}\rvert_{G}^{2}\cdot\langle v\rangle_{G}^{2\alpha}\,{\text{{vol}}}_{\underline{G}}=\int_{M_{t}}\lvert\mathfrak{T}\rvert_{G}^{2}\int_{T^{\ast}_{x}M_{t}}\left(\langle v\rangle_{G}^{2\alpha}\mu_{G}^{-1}dv\right){\text{{vol}}}_{G}\lesssim\|\mathfrak{T}\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M_{t})}^{2}\cdot a^{-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\,.

We use this in the sequel without further comment, usually when needing to estimate high order terms of
G-\gamma,\,\Sigma,\,N
or scalar field quantities.

Next, we collect the core estumate that ensures the transport equation almost conserves Vlasov energies:

Lemma 6.2

(Energy mechanism for the Vlasov equation)
.

Let
\mu\geq 0
and
0\leq L\leq 19
. Then, the following holds:

\displaystyle\int_{T^{\ast}M_{t}}\langle v\rangle^{2\mu}\langle\bm{X}\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L}(f-f_{FLRW}),\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L}(f-f_{FLRW})\rangle_{\underline{G}_{0}}{\text{{vol}}}_{\underline{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon a^{1-c\sigma}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{\mu,0}(f,\cdot)

Similarly, if one additionally has
1\leq K\leq L
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{T^{\ast}M_{t}}\langle v\rangle^{2\mu}\langle\bm{X}\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K}f,\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K}f\rangle_{\underline{G}_{0}}{\text{{vol}}}_{\underline{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon a^{1-c\sigma}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{\mu,K}(f,\cdot)\,.

Proof.

Both estimates are proved identically, so we only write out the proof for the second. Writing out
\bm{X}
(see (
2.19b
)), the left hand side takes the following form:

\displaystyle\int_{T^{\ast}M_{t}}\langle v\rangle_{G}^{2\mu}(v^{0})^{2(L-K)}\left\langle\left[-a^{-1}\frac{v^{\sharp j}}{v^{0}}(N+1)\underline{\nabla}_{j}+a^{-1}v^{0}\nabla_{j}N\,\mathbf{B}^{j}\right]\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K}f,\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K}f\right\rangle_{\underline{G}}\,{\text{{vol}}}_{\underline{G}}

Regarding the first term, we integrate by parts with respect to
\underline{\nabla}_{j}
, noting that the horizontal covariant derivative of any momentum weight vanishes. For the remaining term, we use Fubini’s theorem to replace the integral by one over
\int_{M}\int_{T^{\ast}_{x}M_{t}}
and then integrate by parts in
\mathbf{B}
. This leads to the following after rearranging and applying (
2.24c
):

\displaystyle\int_{T^{\ast}M_{t}}

\displaystyle\,\langle v\rangle_{G}^{2\mu}\langle\bm{X}\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L}(f-f_{FLRW}),\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L}(f-f_{FLRW})\rangle_{\underline{G}_{0}}{\text{{vol}}}_{\underline{G}}

\displaystyle=\int_{T^{\ast}M_{t}}

\displaystyle\,a^{-1}\langle v\rangle^{2\mu}_{G}\,\left(v^{\sharp j}\nabla_{j}N\right)\,v^{0}\left(2\mu\langle v\rangle_{G}^{-2}+2(L-K)(v^{0})^{-2}\right)\lvert\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K}f\rvert_{\underline{G}_{0}}^{2}\,{\text{{vol}}}_{\underline{G}}

The statement then follows using (
3.14
).
∎

To be able to apply the above identity, we need to commute
\partial_{t}
past the respective covariant Sasaki derivatives, apply (
2.19a
), and then commute
\bm{X}
back past them. We have already collected error term formulas for the former in Lemma
4.12
, and now collect schematic formulas for the latter:

Lemma 6.3

(The commuted rescaled Vlasov equation)
.

The following commuted analogues of (
2.19a
) hold: For
1\leq L\leq 19
, one has

(6.1a)

\displaystyle\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L}\partial_{t}f=

\displaystyle\,\bm{X}\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L}(f-f_{FLRW})+a^{-1}(N+1)\frac{(v^{0})^{2}+v\ast v}{(v^{0})^{3}}\ast\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-1}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}(f-f_{FLRW})

\displaystyle\,+a^{-1}(N+1)\frac{v\ast v}{v^{0}}\ast\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-1}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}(f-f_{FLRW})

\displaystyle\,+a^{-1}\frac{v}{v^{0}}\ast\nabla N\ast\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L}(f-f_{FLRW})

\displaystyle\,+\mathfrak{F}_{L,0}+\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L}\bm{X}f_{FLRW}

where one has, for
L\geq 2
,

(6.1b)

\|(v^{0})^{L}\mathfrak{F}_{L,0}\|_{L^{2}_{1,\underline{G}}(T^{\ast}M)}\lesssim a^{-1-c\sigma}\left(\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-1)}_{1,\leq 1}(f,\cdot)}+\|\Gamma-\hat{\Gamma}\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M)}+\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{H^{1}_{G}(M)}\right)

and said error term vanishes for
L=1
, as well as

(6.2)

\displaystyle\|(v^{0})^{L}\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L}

\displaystyle\bm{X}f_{FLRW}\|_{L^{2}_{1,\underline{G}}(T^{\ast}M)}\lesssim a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left(\sqrt{a^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(1)}(N,\cdot)}+\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(N,\cdot)}\right)

\displaystyle\,+a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left(\|\Gamma-\hat{\Gamma}\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M)}+\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M)}\right)\,.

The latter bound also extends to
L=0
.

Furthermore, for
0<K<L
, we have

(6.3a)

\displaystyle\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K}\partial_{t}f=

\displaystyle\,\bm{X}\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K}f+a^{-1}(N+1)\nabla^{K-1}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K+1}\left(\frac{v\ast v}{v^{0}}f\right)

\displaystyle\,+a^{-1}(N+1)\left(\frac{1}{v^{0}}+\frac{v\ast v}{(v^{0})^{3}}\right)\ast\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K-1}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K+1}f

\displaystyle+a^{-1}\frac{v}{v^{0}}\ast\nabla N\ast\mathbf{B}\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K-1}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K}f\phantom{\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K}\partial_{t}f}

\displaystyle\,+\mathfrak{F}_{L,K}

with

(6.3b)

\displaystyle\|(v^{0})^{L-K}

\displaystyle\mathfrak{F}_{L,K}\|_{L^{2}_{1,\underline{G}}(T^{\ast}M)}\lesssim a^{-1-c\sigma}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-1)}_{1,\leq K}(f,\cdot)}+a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq K+1)}(N,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\underbrace{\sqrt{\varepsilon}a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left(\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq K-1)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}+\|\Gamma-\hat{\Gamma}\|_{H^{1}_{G}(M)}+\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{H^{1}_{G}(M)}\right)}_{\text{if }K\geq 2}

as well as

(6.4a)

\displaystyle\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{L}\partial_{t}f=

\displaystyle\,\bm{X}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{L}f+a^{-1}(N+1)\nabla^{L-1}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\underline{\nabla}_{vert}\left(\frac{v\ast v}{v^{0}}f\right)+a^{-1}\frac{v}{v^{0}}\nabla N\ast\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{L}f+\mathfrak{F}_{L,L}

with

(6.4b)

\displaystyle\|

\displaystyle\mathfrak{F}_{L,L}\|_{L^{2}_{1,\underline{G}}(T^{\ast}M)}\lesssim\varepsilon a^{-1-c\sigma}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,\leq L-1}(f,\cdot)}+a^{-1-c\sigma}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-1)}_{1,\leq L-1}(f,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{a^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(L+1)}(N,\cdot)}+a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L)}(N,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\underbrace{\sqrt{\varepsilon}a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left(\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}+\|\Gamma-\hat{\Gamma}\|_{H^{1}_{G}(M)}+\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{H^{1}_{G}(M)}\right)}_{\text{ if }L\geq 2}.

Proof.

We first consider the purely vertical case (
6.1a
), then the purely horizontal case (
6.4a
), and then briefly (
6.3a
) as a combination of both cases before discussing the respective error bounds.

For (
6.1a
), we first replace
f
with
(f-f_{FLRW})+f_{FLRW}
, which allows us to collect all reference error terms in
\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L}\bm{X}f_{FLRW}
. Since
\mathbf{B}_{i}
and
\underline{\nabla}_{i+3}
coincide when acting on purely vertical tensors (see (
2.17
)) and vertical frame derivatives commute, the only error terms generated from the vertical term in computing (
6.1a
) are down to applying the product rule to momentum prefactors. Regarding the horizontal term in
\bm{X}
, we additionally need to apply (
4.24a
) for
M_{2}=0
, which yields the term in the second line of (
6.1a
) as the leading error term, while all others are absorbed into the error terms. In particular, we note that this commutation only generates terms of the type in the second line of (
6.1a
), where we can always pull the horizontal derivative back to the front up to error terms as in Lemma
4.13
that are absorbed into
\mathfrak{F}_{L,0}
. We also note that, whenever vertical derivatives hit
v
-prefactors, both a power of
v
and a derivative are lost, and thus the momentum scaling of lower order error terms always precisely matches that of the respective
\underline{G}_{0}
-norm, up to estimating
v
by
v^{0}
in
\lvert\cdot\rvert_{G}
.

For (
6.4a
), we need to ensure that, after taking horizontal derivatives of
\bm{X}f
and applying the product rule, we can still express all terms covariantly despite Christoffel symbols occuring explicitly in the commutator
[\mathbf{A}_{i},\mathbf{B}^{j}]f=-\Gamma[G]^{j}_{il}\mathbf{B}^{l}f
, see (
2.25b
). To this end, we commute horizontal derivatives past the vertical part of the operator
\bm{X}
as follows.

\displaystyle\underline{\nabla}_{i}\left[v^{0}\nabla_{j}N\mathbf{B}^{j}f\right]=

\displaystyle\,v^{0}\mathbf{A}_{j}\left[\nabla_{j}N\mathbf{B}^{j}f\right]=\,v^{0}\left[\partial_{i}\nabla_{j}N\,\mathbf{B}^{j}f+\nabla_{j}N\,\mathbf{A}_{i}\mathbf{B}^{j}f\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,v^{0}\left[\partial_{i}\nabla_{j}N\,\mathbf{B}^{j}f+\nabla_{j}N\,\left(\mathbf{B}^{j}\mathbf{A}_{i}f-\Gamma[G]^{j}_{il}\mathbf{B}^{l}f\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,v^{0}\left[\nabla_{i}\nabla_{j}N\,\mathbf{B}^{j}f+\nabla_{j}N\mathbf{B}^{j}\underline{\nabla}_{i}f\right]

At higher orders, one additionally incurs terms of the form
v\ast\text{{Riem}}[G]
from connection coefficients with two horizontal indices. Thus, repeating this argument, we obtain the following schematic representation:

\displaystyle\,[\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{L},v^{0}\nabla_{j}N\,\mathbf{B}^{j}f]f

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\sum_{K_{N}+K_{f}=L,\,K_{f}\neq L}v\ast\nabla^{K_{N}+1}N\ast\mathbf{B}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K_{f}}f

\displaystyle\,+\sum_{K_{\text{{Ric}}}+K_{N}+K_{f}=L-2}v\ast\nabla^{K_{\text{{Ric}}}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{K_{N}+1}N\ast\mathbf{B}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K_{f}}f

\displaystyle\,+\langle\text{low order nonlinear error terms with multiple curvature terms}\rangle

Regarding the horizontal term in
\bm{X}f
, we apply (
4.24a
) with
M_{1}=0,M_{2}=L
, again noting that we can rearrange the covariant derivatives up to error terms into the order presented, and can deal with the error terms as in Lemma
4.13
. Finally, (
6.3a
) follows from combining the ideas from both of these proofs, commuting with horizontal derivative terms as in the purely vertical case and noting that
\mathbf{B}
and
\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K}
commute.

The error bounds (
6.4b
), (
6.3b
) and (
6.1b
) simply follow
L^{2}-L^{\infty}-
estimating the remaining terms and using (
4.16
) for low order Vlasov terms, (
4.3e
) for the curvature terms and (
4.6a
) to control momentum terms as well as the lapse bootstrap assumption from (
3.13a
). In particular, we note that
\mathbf{B}f
and higher order vertical derivatives of it are only bounded by
a^{-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}
, leading to the lapse energies in the second line of (
6.4b
). Furthermore, we apply (
4.26b
) to order the derivatives in the Vlasov error terms as needed, and note that the leading curvature terms arise from this procedure.

Finally, for (
6.2
), we recall that
v^{0}
,
\langle v\rangle_{G}
and
\mathbf{B}f_{FLRW}
are bounded by
a^{-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}
on the support of
f_{FLRW}
since the latter has bounded support with respect to
\gamma
and one has (
4.3c
). Using (
4.12
) for the horizontal term in
\bm{X}
then directly leads to the stated bound.
∎

6.2.
Vlasov energy estimates

Recalling our conventions on
t
and
\omega
from the start of this section, we now start by proving the Vlasov integral energy estimate at order
0
:

Lemma 6.4

(Zero order Vlasov energy estimates)
.

The following estimate holds:

(6.5a)

\displaystyle a(t)^{\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}_{1,0}(f,t)\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon^{4}+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(a(s)^{-3+\frac{\omega}{4}}+\varepsilon\,a^{-3}\right)\cdot a(s)^{\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}_{1,0}(f,s)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\frac{\omega}{4}}\left(\varepsilon^{2}\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\Sigma,s)+\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\phi,s)+a(s)^{4+\frac{\omega}{2}}\|\Gamma-\hat{\Gamma}\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M_{s})}^{2}\right)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\frac{\omega}{4}}\cdot a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{2}}\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{L_{G}^{2}(M_{s})}^{2}\,ds

(6.5b)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}_{1,0}(f,t)\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon^{4}+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(a(s)^{-1-c\sigma}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{8}}a(s)^{-3}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}_{1,0}(f,s)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{8}}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left(\varepsilon^{2}\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\Sigma,s)+\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\phi,s)\right)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left(\|\Gamma-\hat{\Gamma}\|_{L_{G}^{2}(M_{s})}^{2}+\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{L_{G}^{2}(M_{s})}^{2}\right)\,ds

Proof.

We first prove the unscaled estimate (
6.5b
) before sketching the necessary adaptations to arrive at (
6.5a
):

(6.6)

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}_{1,0}(f,\cdot)=

\displaystyle\,-\int_{T^{\ast}M}2\frac{\partial_{t}\langle v\rangle_{G}}{\langle v\rangle_{G}}\,\langle v\rangle^{2}_{G}\lvert f-f_{FLRW}\rvert^{2}\,{\text{{vol}}}_{\underline{G}}

\displaystyle\,-2\int_{T^{\ast}M}\langle v\rangle_{G}^{2}(f-f_{FLRW})\cdot\partial_{t}f\,{\text{{vol}}}_{\underline{G}}

The first line can be bounded by
\lesssim\varepsilon a^{-3}\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}_{1,0}(f,\cdot)
with (
4.23
) and (
3.13a
). We insert the rescaled Vlasov equation (
2.19a
) in the second line and expand as follows:

\displaystyle\,-2\int_{T^{\ast}M}\langle v\rangle_{G}^{2}(f-f_{FLRW})\cdot\partial_{t}f\,{\text{{vol}}}_{\underline{G}}

(6.7a)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,-2\int_{T^{\ast}M}\langle v\rangle_{G}^{2}(f-f_{FLRW})\cdot\bm{X}(f-f_{FLRW})\,{\text{{vol}}}_{\underline{G}}

(6.7b)

\displaystyle\,-2\int_{T^{\ast}M}\langle v\rangle_{G}^{2}(f-f_{FLRW})\bm{X}f_{FLRW}

(
6.7a
) is bounded by
\varepsilon a^{-3}\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}_{1,0}(f,\cdot)
due to core energy mechanism from Lemma
6.2
. Regarding (
6.7b
), we apply Lemma
5.4
for
L=0
to (
6.2
) and collect:

\displaystyle\|\bm{X}f_{FLRW}\|_{L^{2}_{1,\underline{G}}(T^{\ast}M)}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left[\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M)}+\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}_{1,0}(f,\cdot)}\right]

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left[\sqrt{\varepsilon^{2}\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}+\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\phi,\cdot)}+a^{4}\|\Gamma-\hat{\Gamma}\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M)}\right]

Putting all of the above together and applying the Young inequality on each summand, we obtain

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}_{1,0}(f,\cdot)\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\left(a^{-1-c\sigma}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{8}}a^{-3}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}_{1,0}(f,\cdot)+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{8}}a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left(\varepsilon^{2}\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\Sigma,\cdot)+\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\phi,\cdot)+a^{4}\|\Gamma-\hat{\Gamma}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\,+a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}^{2}

and thus the result after applying the Gronwall lemma.

For (
6.5a
), we argue along the same lines, with the estimates for (
6.6
) and (
6.7a
) treated identically and where
(-\partial_{t}a^{\omega})\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(f,\cdot)
can be dropped since it is nonpositive. Regarding the terms in (
6.7b
), we apply the same estimates, but now distribute the additional Vlasov energy scaling to mitigate the divergence of the other energies:

\displaystyle\,a^{-3}\cdot\left(a^{\frac{\omega}{4}}\|\bm{X}f_{FLRW}\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{G}}}\right)\cdot\left(a^{\frac{3\omega}{4}}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}_{1,0}^{(0)}(f,\cdot)}\right)\,

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,a^{-3+\frac{\omega}{4}}\left(a^{\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}_{1,0}(f,\cdot)\right)+a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\frac{\omega}{4}}\left(\varepsilon^{2}\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\Sigma,\cdot)+\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\phi,\cdot)+a^{4+\frac{\omega}{2}}\|\Gamma-\hat{\Gamma}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{G}}\right)

\displaystyle\,+a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\frac{\omega}{4}}\cdot a^{\frac{\omega}{2}}\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}^{2}\,.

∎

Lemma 6.5

(Vertical Vlasov energy estimates)
.

Let
\beta\in\{0,4\}
. Then:

(6.8)

\displaystyle a(t)^{\beta+\omega}

\displaystyle\,\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,0}(f,t)\lesssim\varepsilon^{4}+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(a(s)^{-1-c\sigma}+a(s)^{-3+\frac{\omega}{4}}+\varepsilon a(s)^{-3}\right)a(s)^{\beta+{\omega}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,0}(f,s)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}a(s)^{-1-c\sigma-{\omega}}\cdot a(s)^{\beta+2{\omega}}\mathfrak{E}_{1,\leq 1}^{(\leq L)}(f,s)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\beta+\frac{\omega}{2}}\left(\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\phi,s)+\varepsilon^{2}a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{4}}\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\Sigma,s)\right)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\beta+\frac{\omega}{4}}\left(a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{2}}\|\Gamma-\hat{\Gamma}\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M_{s})}^{2}+a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{2}}\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M_{s})}^{2}\right)\,ds

(6.9)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,0}(f,t)\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon^{4}+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(a(s)^{-1-c\sigma}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{8}}a(s)^{-3}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,0}(f,s)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}a(s)^{-1-c\sigma-2\omega}\cdot a(s)^{2\omega}\mathfrak{E}_{1,\leq 1}^{(\leq L)}(f,s)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{8}}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\beta}\left(\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\phi,s)+\varepsilon^{2}\mathfrak{E}^{(0)}(\Sigma,s)\right)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left(\|\Gamma-\hat{\Gamma}\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M_{s})}^{2}+\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M_{s})}^{2}\right)\,ds

Proof.

We only prove (
6.9
) in full since the other cases are proven similarly, keeping in mind that
-\partial_{t}(a^{\beta+\omega})\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,0}\leq 0
.

Since
\partial_{t}
and
\mathbf{B}_{i}
commute and since
\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L}\xi=\mathbf{B}^{L}\xi
holds, we get the following:

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,0}(f,\cdot)=

\displaystyle\,-\int_{T^{\ast}M}\langle v\rangle_{G}^{2}\left(2\frac{\partial_{t}\langle v\rangle_{G}}{\langle v\rangle_{G}}+2L\frac{\partial_{t}v^{0}}{v^{0}}\right)\lvert\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L}(f-f_{FLRW})\rvert^{2}_{\underline{G}_{0}}{\text{{vol}}}_{\underline{G}}

\displaystyle\,-\int_{T^{\ast}M}\langle v\rangle_{G}^{2}(v^{0})^{2L}\partial_{t}G\ast_{\underline{G}}\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L}(f-f_{FLRW})\ast_{\underline{G}}^{L}\underline{\nabla}_{vert}(f-f_{FLRW})\,{\text{{vol}}}_{\underline{G}}

\displaystyle\,-2\int_{T^{\ast}M}\langle v\rangle_{G}^{2}\langle\underline{\nabla}_{vert}(f-f_{FLRW}),\underline{\nabla}_{vert}\partial_{t}f\rangle_{\underline{G}_{0}}\,{\text{{vol}}}_{\underline{G}}

The first two lines are bounded by
\varepsilon a^{-3}\mathfrak{E}^{(1)}_{1,0}(f,\cdot)
up to constant, with the first line due to (
4.23
) and (
3.14
), and with the second since
\lvert\partial_{t}G\rvert_{G}\lesssim\varepsilon a^{-3}
holds by (
4.1b
) and (
3.14
) applied to (
2.20a
). Regarding the final line, we insert (
6.1a
):

\displaystyle\,-2\int_{T^{\ast}M}\langle v\rangle_{G}^{2}\langle\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L}(f-f_{FLRW}),\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L}\partial_{t}f\rangle_{\underline{G}_{0}}\,{\text{{vol}}}_{\underline{G}}

(6.10a)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,-2\int_{T^{\ast}M}\langle v\rangle_{G}^{2}(v^{0})^{2L}\langle\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L}(f-f_{FLRW}),\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L}\bm{X}f_{FLRW})\rangle_{\underline{G}}{\text{{vol}}}_{\underline{G}}

(6.10b)

\displaystyle+\int_{T^{\ast}M}\langle v\rangle_{G}^{2}\left[a^{-1}\lvert N+1\rvert\left(1+\lvert v\rvert_{G}^{2}\lvert\text{{Ric}}[G]\rvert_{G}\right)\lvert\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-1}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}(f-f_{FLRW})\rvert_{\underline{G}_{0}}\right.

(6.10c)

\displaystyle\quad\left.+a^{-1}\lvert\nabla N\rvert_{G}\lvert\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L}(f-f_{FLRW})\rvert_{\underline{G}_{0}}\right]\lvert\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L}(f-f_{FLRW})\rvert_{\underline{G}_{0}}\,{\text{{vol}}}_{\underline{G}}

(6.10d)

\displaystyle\,+\left(\|\mathfrak{F}_{L,0}\|_{L^{2}_{1,\underline{G}}}+\|\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L}\bm{X}f_{FLRW}\|_{L^{2}_{1,\underline{G}}}\right)\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,0}(f,\cdot)}

Going through the right hand side line by line, (
6.10a
) can be bounded by
\varepsilon a^{-3}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,0}(f,\cdot)
by Lemma
6.2
.
For (
6.10b
), we apply (
4.3e
) and (
4.6a
) on the prefactor to estimate it as follows:

\lvert\eqref{eq:vlasov-vert-en2}\rvert\lesssim a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left(\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,1}(f,\cdot)}+\|\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-1}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}f_{FLRW}\|_{L^{2}_{1,\underline{G}_{0}}}\right)\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,0}(f,\cdot)}

For the reference term, we apply (
4.11a
) for
K=1
, and obtain the bound

\lvert\eqref{eq:vlasov-vert-en2}\rvert\lesssim a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,1}(f,\cdot)+a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left(\|\Gamma-\hat{\Gamma}\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M_{t})}^{2}+\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M_{t})}^{2}\right)+a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,0}(f,\cdot)\,.

For (
6.10c
), we apply (
4.6a
) and (
3.14
) to bound the terms by
\varepsilon a^{1-c\sigma}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,0}
. Finally, we apply (
6.1b
) and (
6.2
) to bound (
6.10d
), where we insert Lemma
5.4
at order
l=0
for the occuring lapse energy terms and then apply the Young inequality. The integral estimate (
6.8
) then follows by integrating.

For
\omega^{\prime}=\omega
, we again use the additional scaling to prevent otherwise strongly divergent energy terms to diverge more than
a^{-3}
as in the zero order case. For the Vlasov energy gaining a horizontal derivative, we distribute the scaling as follows:

a^{\omega}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,0}(f,\cdot)}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,1}(f,\cdot)}=\sqrt{a^{\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,0}(f,\cdot)}\cdot\sqrt{a^{-\omega}\cdot a^{2\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,1}(f,\cdot)}

The respective proofs with
\beta=4
instead of
\beta=0
are identical.
∎

Lemma 6.6

(Mixed Vlasov energy estimates)
.

Let
0<K<L
and
\beta\in\{0,4\}
. Then, the following estimates hold:

(6.11)

\displaystyle a(t)^{\beta+(K+1){\omega}}

\displaystyle\,\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,K}(f,t)\lesssim\,\varepsilon^{4}+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(a(s)^{-3+\frac{\omega}{4}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{8}}a(s)^{-3}\right)a(s)^{\beta+(K+1){\omega}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,K}(f,s)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}a(s)^{-1-{\omega}}\cdot a(s)^{\beta+(K+2){\omega}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,K+1}(f,s)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\frac{\omega}{2}}+\varepsilon^{2}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\frac{\omega}{2}}\right)\cdot a(s)^{\beta+((K-1)+1){\omega}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L)}_{1,\leq K-1}(f,s)

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\frac{\omega}{2}}a(s)^{\beta}\left(a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{2}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq K)}(\Sigma,s)+\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq K)}(\phi,s)\right)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+K{\omega}}+\varepsilon a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+K{\omega}}\right)\cdot a(s)^{\beta+\frac{\omega}{2}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq K-1)}(\text{{Ric}},s)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\frac{(2K+1){\omega}}{2}}+\varepsilon a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+K\omega}\right)\cdot

\displaystyle\,\phantom{\int_{t}^{t_{0}}}\quad\cdot\left(a(s)^{\beta+\frac{\omega}{2}}\|\Gamma-\hat{\Gamma}\|_{H^{1}_{G}(M_{s})}^{2}+a(s)^{\beta+\frac{\omega}{2}}\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M_{s})}^{2}\right)\,ds

(6.12)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,K}(f,t)\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon^{4}+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(a(s)^{-1-c\sigma}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{8}}a(s)^{-3}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,K}(f,s)+a(s)^{-1}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,K+1}(f,s)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{8}}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\left(\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,K-1}(f,s)+\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-1)}_{1,\leq K-1}(f,s)\right)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{8}}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left(\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq K)}(\Sigma,s)+\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq K)}(\phi,s)\right)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{15}{8}}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq K-1)}(\text{{Ric}},s)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{15}{8}}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\left(\|\Gamma-\hat{\Gamma}\|_{H^{1}_{G}(M_{s})}^{2}+\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{G}(M_{s})}^{2}\right)\,ds

For
K<10
, the curvature energies can be dropped.

Proof.

We again focus on proving the unscaled energy inequality (
6.12
), and touch on how the scale factors are distributed for (
6.11
) at the end. One computes:

\displaystyle\,-\partial_{t}\int_{T^{\ast}M}\lvert v\rvert_{G}^{2}\lvert\underline{\nabla}^{L-K}_{vert}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K}f\rvert_{\underline{G}_{0}}^{2}\,{\text{{vol}}}_{\underline{G}}

(6.13a)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,-\int_{T^{\ast}M}\langle v\rangle_{G}^{2}\left(2\frac{\partial_{t}\langle v\rangle_{G}}{\langle v\rangle_{G}}+2(L-K)\frac{\partial_{t}v^{0}}{v^{0}}\right)\lvert\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K}f\rvert^{2}_{\underline{G}_{0}}{\text{{vol}}}_{\underline{G}}

(6.13b)

\displaystyle\,-\int_{T^{\ast}M}\langle v\rangle_{G}^{2}(v^{0})^{2(L-K)}(\partial_{t}\underline{G}^{-1})\ast_{\underline{G}}\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K}f\ast_{\underline{G}}\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K}f\,{\text{{vol}}}_{\underline{G}}

(6.13c)

\displaystyle\,-2\int_{T^{\ast}M}\langle v\rangle_{G}^{2}\langle[\partial_{t},\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K}]f,\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K}f\rangle_{\underline{G}_{0}}\,{\text{{vol}}}_{\underline{G}}

(6.13d)

\displaystyle\,-2\int_{T^{\ast}M}\langle v\rangle_{G}^{2}\langle\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K}\partial_{t}f,\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K}f\rangle_{\underline{G}_{0}}\,{\text{{vol}}}_{\underline{G}}

(
6.13a
)-(
6.13b
) can be bounded by
\varepsilon a^{-3}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,K}(f,\cdot)
up to constant as in the purely vertical case. Regarding (
6.13c
), (
4.24b
) implies the following:

\displaystyle\,\left\lvert\langle[\partial_{t},\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K}]f,\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K}f\rangle_{\underline{G}_{0}}\right\rvert

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\lvert\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K}f\rvert_{\underline{G}_{0}}\cdot\left[\frac{\lvert v\rvert_{G}}{v^{0}}\lvert\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K+1}f\rvert_{\underline{G}_{0}}\lvert\nabla^{K-2}\partial_{t}\text{{Ric}}[G]\rvert_{G}\right.

\displaystyle\,\left.+\frac{\lvert v\rvert_{G}}{v^{0}}\lvert\partial_{t}\Gamma[G]\rvert_{G}\lvert\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K+1}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K-1}f\rvert_{\underline{G}_{0}}+(v^{0})^{L-K}\lvert\Xi_{L-K,K,t}f\rvert_{\underline{G}}\right]

Recall that, by integration by parts and the Young inequality, one has

a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(l-1)}(\Sigma,\cdot)\lesssim a^{-3}\mathfrak{E}^{(l)}(\Sigma,\cdot)+a^{-3-2c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(l-2)}(\Sigma,\cdot)

and the analogous estimates for
\mathfrak{E}^{(l-1)}(N,\cdot)
and
\mathfrak{E}^{(l-1)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)
. Applying (
4.3b
) and (
3.14
) to (
2.20e
) and (
2.20d
), as well as updating
c>0
, one then gets

\displaystyle\,\|\partial_{t}\text{{Ric}}[G]\|_{H^{l-2}_{G}}+\|\partial_{t}\Gamma[G]\|_{H^{l-1}_{G}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,a^{-3}\left(\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(l)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}+\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(l)}(N,\cdot)}\right)+a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left(\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq l-2)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}+\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq l-2)}(N,\cdot)}\right)

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq l-2)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}

as well as

\|\partial_{t}\text{{Ric}}[G]\|_{C^{10}_{G}(M)}+\|\partial_{t}\Gamma[G]\|_{C^{11}_{G}(M)}\lesssim\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\,.

Further, the supremum norm over
\xi=f
in (
4.25b
) can be bounded by
a^{-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}
due to (
4.16
) whenever it occurs. Altogether, this implies

\displaystyle\lvert\eqref{eq:vlasov-interim-3}\rvert\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left(\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,K-1}(f,\cdot)}+\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-1)}_{1,\leq K-1}(f,\cdot)}\right)

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left(\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq K)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}+\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq K)}(N,\cdot)}+\varepsilon\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq K-2)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}\right)\,.

Considering (
6.13d
), we can apply (
6.3a
) and using
\lvert v\rvert_{G}\leq v^{0}
as well as
\lvert N+1\rvert\lesssim 1
to obtain the following:

\displaystyle\eqref{eq:vlasov-interim-4}+

\displaystyle\,2\int_{T^{\ast}M}\langle v\rangle_{G}^{2}\langle\bm{X}\underline{\nabla}^{L-K}_{vert}\underline{\nabla}^{K}_{hor}f,\underline{\nabla}^{L-K}_{vert}\underline{\nabla}^{K}_{hor}f\rangle{\text{{vol}}}_{\underline{G}}

(6.14a)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\int_{T^{\ast}M}\langle v\rangle_{G}^{2}\left[a^{-1}\lvert\nabla^{K-1}\text{{Ric}}[G]\rvert_{G}\cdot(v^{0})^{2(L-K)}\left\lvert\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K+1}\left(\frac{v\ast v}{v^{0}}f\right)\right\rvert_{\underline{G}}+a^{-1}\lvert\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K-1}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K+1}f\rvert_{\underline{G}_{0}}\right.

(6.14b)

\displaystyle\,+a^{-1}(v^{0})^{L-K}\lvert\nabla N\rvert_{G}\lvert\mathbf{B}\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K-1}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K}f\rvert_{\underline{G}}+\left.(v^{0})^{L-K}\lvert\mathfrak{F}_{L,K}\rvert_{\underline{G}}\right]\cdot\lvert\underline{\nabla}^{L-K}_{vert}\underline{\nabla}^{K}_{hor}f\rvert_{\underline{G}_{0}}\,{\text{{vol}}}_{\underline{G}}

The extra term on the left hand side is dealt with using Lemma
6.2
. Using (
4.6a
) and (
4.16
), the vertical derivative factor in the first term of (
6.14a
) can be estimated by
a^{-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}
. Regarding the first term in (
6.14b
), note that one schematically has

\mathbf{B}\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K-1}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K}f=\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K}f+\Gamma[G]\ast\underline{\nabla}^{L-1}f,

where the second term always contains horizontal derivatives except for the case
K=1
. Then, one expands the term as follows:

\lvert(v^{0})^{L-1}\Gamma[G]\ast\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-1}f\rvert_{G}\lesssim a^{-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left(\lvert\underline{\nabla}^{L-1}_{vert}(f-f_{FLRW})\rvert_{\underline{G}_{0}}+\lvert\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-1}f_{FLRW}\rvert_{\underline{G}_{0}}\right)\,.

The former can be related back to
\mathfrak{E}^{(L-1)}_{1,0}(f,\cdot)
in estimation and the latter is simply bounded uniformly by
a^{-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}
. Else, this lower order term can be bounded by
\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(L-1)}_{1,\leq K-1}(f,\cdot)}
in estimation.
We can treat
\mathbf{B}\underline{\nabla}_{vert}^{L-K}\underline{\nabla}_{hor}^{K-1}f
identically, except that the case of only vertical derivatives appearing in error terms now happens for
K\leq 2
.
Thus and applying (
4.3e
) and (
4.16
) as well as (
3.13a
) for the lapse, we obtain

\displaystyle\eqref{eq:vlasov-interim-4}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\left(a^{-1-c\sigma}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{8}}a^{-3}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,K}(f,\cdot)+a^{-1}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,K+1}(f,\cdot)+\varepsilon^{\frac{15}{8}}a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,K-1}(f,\cdot)

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-1)}_{1,\leq K-1}(f,\cdot)+a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(K-1)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)+a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}{a^{4}\mathfrak{E}^{(1)}(N,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\|(v^{0})^{(L-K)}\mathfrak{F}_{L,K}\|_{L^{2}_{1,\underline{G}}}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,K}(f,\cdot)}

Note that the last term in the second line comes from the discussed error terms arising from replacing
\mathbf{B}
with
\underline{\nabla}_{vert}
at low horizontal orders.

The statement now follows by inserting (
6.3b
), combining the bounds for (
6.13a
)-(
6.13d
), inserting Lemma
5.4
and integrating.

For the scaled version, the main point is that one needs to be careful to ensure that
\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,K-1}(f,\cdot)
terms do not enter at a power worse than
a^{-3}
, which is the case in the unscaled version. To this end, we distribute the scaling as follows:

a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+(K+1){\omega}}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,K}(f,\cdot)}\sqrt{\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,K-1}(f,\cdot)}=a^{-3+\frac{\omega}{2}}\sqrt{a^{(K+1)\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,K}(f,\cdot)}\sqrt{a^{K\omega-2c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,K}(f,\cdot)}

Then, we apply the Young inequality and update
c
. We note that one never encounters more than
K
horizontal derivatives in the error terms and thus can never lose scaling. For the non-Vlasov energies, we distribute scaling as in the previous arguments. Again, replacing
\beta=0
with
\beta=4
goes through identically.
∎

Lemma 6.7

(Horizontal Vlasov energy estimates)
.

Let
\beta\in\{0,4\}
and
L\geq 2
. Then:

(6.15)

\displaystyle a(t)^{\beta+(L+1)\omega}

\displaystyle\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,L}(f,t)\lesssim\varepsilon^{4}+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(a(s)^{-3+\frac{\omega}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{8}}a(s)^{-3}\right)a(s)^{\beta+(L+1)\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,L}(f,s)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\varepsilon a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\frac{\omega}{2}}\cdot a(s)^{\beta+L{\omega}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L)}_{1,\leq L-1}(f,s)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\omega}\cdot a(s)^{\beta}\left(a(s)^{\frac{\omega}{4}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L)}(\Sigma,s)+\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L)}(\phi,s)\right)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\frac{(2L-1){\omega}}{2}}\cdot a(s)^{4+\beta+\omega}\mathfrak{E}^{(L-1)}(\text{{Ric}},s)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+L\omega}\,a(s)^{\beta+\frac{\omega}{2}}\mathfrak{E}^{(L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},s)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(\varepsilon a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\omega}+a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\,a(s)^{\beta+\frac{\omega}{2}}\mathfrak{E}^{(\leq L-4)}(\text{{Ric}},s)\,ds

\displaystyle\,+\int_{t}^{t_{0}}\left(a(s)^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\frac{(2L+1){\omega}}{2}}+\varepsilon a(s)^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}+L\omega}\right)\cdot

\displaystyle\,\phantom{\int_{t}^{t_{0}}}\quad\cdot\left(a(s)^{\beta+\frac{\omega}{2}}\|\Gamma-\hat{\Gamma}\|_{H^{1}_{G}(M_{s})}^{2}+a(s)^{\beta+\frac{\omega}{2}}\|G^{-1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{H^{2}_{G}(M_{s})}^{2}\right)\,ds

(6.16)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{E}^{(L)}_{1,L}(f,t)\lesssim
